As if stimulated by the dragon’s gaze, several large clam shells in the sand pool slowly opened, revealing the pearls inside. Suddenly, the entire deep pool became clearer and brighter, as if a light had suddenly turned on.

The old dragon’s two huge eyes converged on this little paper crane. He observed it as he asked his daughter more questions.

“This is a letter from Ji Yuan? He folded a paper bird? Was there anything else?”

“To answer you, Father, this paper bird flew into the hand of my statue in the temple. There was nothing else aside from this, and this bird can truly fly.”

As the Dragon Daughter spoke this sentence, she stretched out her hands to either side and mimed the flapping of wings, indicating that this bird was not floating through the air by absorbing spiritual energy, but instead was able to fly by flapping its own wings. There was a fundamental difference between these two actions.

“Interesting, Ji Yuan always brings surprises!”

The Dragon Daughter smiled, and reminded her father.

“Father, the hair around the paper bird’s neck is some kind of message activation technique. You can read the letter by removing the hair. Your daughter didn’t know about this before, and accidentally read it it first. Please don’t blame me, ok?”

The true dragon glanced at the Dragon Daughter, and his huge body glowed with white light. It shrank in an illusion and transformed into an old man, Ying Hong’s human form.

“Let me just take a look at it.”

As he spoke, the Old Dragon lightly stretched out a hand, and the hair on the paper crane’s neck automatically floated to the side.

At this moment, the paper crane actually flapped its wings twice, and approached the old dragon in the air bubble.

“Wow, it really can fly by itself.”

The Old Dragon smiled and stretched out his hand, letting the paper crane, which had been moving through the water with difficulty, fall into his palm. The remaining spiritual energy on the paper crane presented all of the contents recorded in its body to the Old Dragon’s mind.

Ying Ruoli concentrated on observing her father’s expression. Sure enough, she saw what she had been expecting after a while.

The left side of the Old Dragon’s beard tilted up slightly. He spoke with a surprised expression on his face.

“Bing Province, Changhuan Prefecture? This little thing flew from so far away?”

One couldn’t guess about how other dragon lineages worked, but the Ying family would often go out to travel, control the clouds, and spread rain. They had a thorough understanding of the geography of Dazhen, and were very clear about the distance between these two locations.

However, after expressing his surprised, the Old Dragon merely smiled at his daughter.

“Okay, I have received the letter. You may take your leave.”

“Alright, this daughter will be going now!”

The Dragon Daughter bowed lightly and left the sand pool where her father had been napping.

After the Dragon Daughter left, the old dragon immediately lowered his head and studied the paper crane in his palm carefully. He couldn’t help but poke it a few times, then picked it up, flipped it up and down, and shook it twice, as if to check whether there was some kind of precious pearl or treasure hidden inside.

“How strange! Could it be because of this hair?”

The old dragon took out Ji Yuan’s hair and inspected it carefully, but still couldn’t detect anything. However, when he let go again, the hair floated out of the cave along with the current. It seemed as though it were drifting with the flow of the water without any force, but it gave off a strange feeling of intentionality, and soon disappeared into the darkness in the depths of the water.

The Old Dragon frowned. He hesitated for a moment but did not move, letting the hair go. He then continued to study the paper crane in his hand.

The spiritual energy used to convey information through objects had disappeared, as if the father and daughter’s two “readings” of this letter had exhausted the power of the paper crane. However, the Old Dragon was of the opinion that this paper crane would likely not turn back into an ordinary piece of paper.

He carefully controlled and transferred some spiritual energy into the paper crane. Sure enough, the paper crane began to flap its wings again. Following the path of his own spiritual energy, he finally discovered the two hidden words on the paper crane’s wings, and also understood the paper bird had been folded.

The old dragon grinned, as if discovering these hidden secrets were quite remarkable achievements.

. . .

At the top of Yun Mountain’s Yanxia Peak, Ji Yuan finished watching the sunrise and completed his training under the morning glow. Then, he descended the peak and returned to Cloud Mountain Temple.

Qi Wen had dressed and washed up, and was now arranging his shoulder pole and buckets at the entrance to the kitchen from the courtyard, preparing to go down the mountain to fetch water. When he turned around, he found Ji Yuan standing in the inner courtyard of the temple.

“Good morning, Mr. Ji! Are you up too? I didn’t hear your door open.”

“Good morning, Taoist Priest Qi Wen. In fact, I got up earlier than you, and I have already gone out for a walk.”

Qi Wen suddenly understood.

“By the way, Mr. Ji, there is porridge cooking in the kitchen pot. It should be ready while I go fetch water from the bottom of the mountain. I’ll be heading out first.”

“Can I be of any help?”

Ji Yuan took a look at the two buckets. They were larger than the buckets he had used in the small pavilion on Tian Nui Lane. It must be very tiring to carry them up the mountain, particularly as there was a very long stretch of mountain trail without any stairs going down from Cloud Mountain Temple.

“No, no, you can just take a seat. I’m used to running errands, so I can make the round trip in no time.”

Qi Wen quickly refused, and hurried out of the yard with the empty buckets.

Ji Yuan walked over and gently closed the courtyard door. He then moved to the kitchen. The charcoal in the stove was burning faintly, and the fire was not very strong. He opened the other kitchen door and glanced at the place where he had hung the fish tail the night before. There was now only a piece of straw rope rope left, which seemed to have had a hole bitten straight through it.

Based on the scenery outside, Cloud Mountain Temple was located just above the cloud line for the mountain range. From Ji Yuan’s current point of view looking down, all he could see was a sea of white clouds only a dozen feet down, making Yunshan Temple truly look like an immortal landscape in the sky. The Taoists who had built Cloud Mountain Temple really knew how to choose an excellent location.

. . .

The pace of life of ordinary people in this world was very slow, and this was particularly true for Cloud Mountain Temple, especially since, in the few days that Taoist Qingsong was drunk and asleep, there was no need for them to go down the mountain to sell their fortune-telling.

However, Qi Wen was still very worried about his master’s health. From the third day onwards, he kept asking Ji Yuan when Taoist Qingsong would wake up.

Fortunately, Qi Xuan woke up on his own in the early morning of the fifth day.

At this time, Qi Wen happened to have gone down the mountain to fetch more water. Ji Yuan was sitting on a cushion under the star map in the main hall of the temple, reading the “Imperial Discourse.” book in his hand. The spiritual energy in this mountain was like a misty fog invisible to the naked eye, that gathered around the entire Cloud Mountain Temple. At this moment, he noticed that Taoist Qingsong was awake.

Taoist Qingsong felt like he had experienced a very long dream, the content of which was so chaotic that he couldn’t remember any of it clearly. When he opened his eyes, the first thing he saw was the ceiling of the room.

“It’s already dawn! Was I really drunk?”

Qi Xuan slept for almost five days, but he didn’t feel lethargic at all; instead he felt refreshed.

He threw back the quilt, stood up from the bed, and stretched at his waist, feeling his muscles and bones pop. He felt relaxed all over, but his mouth was quite dry.

He walked to the table inside the room and weighed the large ceramic teapot in one hand. It was full.

Qi Xuan poured himself a cup of tea and drank it in one gulp. Still feeling thirsty, he poured himself another cup. Finally, he tipped the spout of the teapot into his mouth and drank all the liquid directly from the teapot in one breath.

“Haaah… I feel much more comfortable! What kind of wine did Mr. Ji give me to drink?”

As he talked to himself, Taoist Qingsong suddenly seemed to think of something. He stretched out his left palm to divine carefully, and his expression changed from dull to astonished.

“Could it be that… immortals really have the elixir of life?”

What Qi Xuan found even more mysterious was that he could clearly see that his lifespan was now much longer, but he could no longer see anything else about his future clearly. It felt like he was looking at flowers through fog; although he could not see them clearly, he knew that there was beautiful scenery in front of him.

“Haha! As far as I know, there is no such thing as an elixir of life in this world. Good morning, Taoist Qingsong!”

Ji Yuan’s clear and peaceful voice came from the door, bringing Taoist Qingsong back to his senses. He turned to the door with a grateful smile.

“Good morning, Mr. Ji. I was too drunk last night, and must have made a fool of myself. Did you rest well last night?”

“I slept pretty well!”

Ji Yuan chuckled.

“I would like to borrow some precious space here to practice for a while. Would you be willing?”

“Of course, feel free to ask for anything. You just stay here, hahaha…”

Anyone would be in a good mood to knew that they would not be dying early. Taoist Qingsong just felt that the air in the mountains was fresher and more pleasant to breathe, as if every breath were invigorating his five internal organs.

T/n: Happy New Year!!!

Chapter 161: The Great Taoist Master

When Qi Wen returned from fetching water to the top of the mountain, he was surprised to see that Taoist Qingsong was awake. He informed him that he had been sleeping for almost five days, and that if he hadn’t woken up, he would have personally carried his master down the mountain to find a doctor, even if Mr. Ji tried to stop him.

At his words, Taoist Qingsong was stunned. Ji Yuan found it funny. Fortunately, Qi Wen had believed in him, and in his estimate of “three to five days” that he had given that day.

However, what puzzled the two Taoists was the fact that, although Taoist Qingsong had been sleeping for several days, apart from being thirsty, he was not very hungry, despite his excellent appetite at lunch that day.

. . .

One afternoon over ten days later, Taoist Qingsong was practicing his health-improving exercises in the Taoist temple. Ji Yuan had dragged out a cushion from the main hall of the temple, and sat cross-legged at the door of the hall, reading “Imperial Discourse.”

Qi Wen had gone down the mountain with a bucket carrying dozens of copper coins. He wanted to buy some meat from a village near the base of the mountain, as the Taoist temple’s stock had been completely consumed.

Their meat selection mainly consisted of cured meats and pickled goods that were easy to preserve in the mountains, as well as some fresh meat. As for vegetables, there was no shortage of them, and there was even quite a bit of rice.

Although Ji Yuan was reading his book, he also carefully observed Taoist Qingsong. After seeing Qi Xuan finish a few rounds of his health-improving cultivation exercises, he glanced at the incense burner outside, smiled, and asked a question.

“Master Qingsong, your incense sales here at Cloud Mountain Temple are not doing that well. I have been here for a few days, and apart from Qi Wen, who lights three incense sticks in the morning, I have not seen any pilgrims outside.”

Taoist Qingsong answered Ji Yuan only after finishing his final posture in the exercise sequence.

“Mr. Ji, you must be joking. Our Taoist temple does not have any statues. What would the common people at the foot of the mountain come up to pray for? Occasionally, a wealthy family will come to ask for a name for their descendants and offer some sacrifices to heaven and earth. This Taoist temple is quite prosperous, by that metric.”

After Qi Xuan finished saying this, he added another sentence.

“Otherwise, why would I always be going down the mountain to tell fortunes? Isn’t it just to make money to maintain the temple?”

“Oh… the Taoist priest is quite principled!”

Ji Yuan gave a meaningful “Oh,” making Taoist Qingsong feel slightly embarrassed.

“Sometimes it’s a bit impulsive, only sometimes…”

Ji Yuan did not push further on this subject, but continued the serious discussion with Qi Xuan.

“Taoist Master, there are no statues of gods in your temple. Logically speaking, there is therefore no need to burn incense all the time. What is the incense for? This sandalwood also costs money.”

“What is it for… Naturally, it is for all the stars in the sky and all of heaven and earth in this world.”

Taoist Qingsong thought about it for a moment. He had been traveling with his own master, just as Qi Wen now did, since he was a child. He had lit incense sticks in any number of temples and Buddhist monasteries. Some Taoist temples that he had encountered even enshrined statues of gods. If he truly had to pinpoint what, or who, Cloud Mountain Temple, which had no statues of gods, was burning incense for, then the answer would naturally be all of heaven and earth.

“But this world doesn’t accept your incense.”

Ji Yuan looked up. As opposed to Qi Xuan’s mortal eyes, he could see the true “incense”.

“Ah? Heaven and earth do not accept it?”

Taoist Qingsong was stunned for a moment. He wanted to say something, but didn’t know where to start. After all, the person sitting in front of the hall was Mr. Ji. However, he was surprised for a brief moment.

“If they don’t accept it, then they simply don’t accept it. Even if heaven and earth do not accept the incense, we can’t be disrespectful!”

“The Taoist priest is right!”

Ji Yuan closed his book and bowed with cupped hands to Taoist Qingsong. Qi Xuan smiled brightly and quickly returned the salute.

. . .

Qi Wen traveled down the mountain with the basket on his back, walking along the mountain trail that the master and disciple often used to pass through Yun Mountain. He crossed the mountain stream, and walked along the ridge. With his swift pace, he left Yun Mountain in just half an hour.

The nearest village at the foot of the mountain was called Yunkou Village, which was often frequented by Taoists from Cloud Mountain Temple. Sometimes, when the village held a wedding or a funeral, or when they needed the help of a fortune-teller or spiritual expert, they would seek help from the Taoists of Cloud Mountain Temple. The two parties were quite familiar with each other.

As soon as he arrived at the village gate, someone greeted Qi Wen.

“Young Taoist Priest Qi Wen, is it just you coming down the mountain? Is Taoist Qingsong not here today?”

“Yeah, Master is still on the mountain. I came to the village to buy some food.”

“Oh… My wife will soon be giving birth again, I would like to ask Taoist Qingsong to calculate their fortune please.”

“Alright!”

Qi Wen greeted the man briefly and entered the village, following the loud sound of chickens and dogs. Some familiar older individuals also greeted Qi Wen. Some of the younger people who had played with Qi Wen when they were young enthusiastically said hello.

As usual, he visited the village chief’s house, along with a few villagers’ houses that he knew well. Like this, he could buy quite a lot with just a few coins.

At an old farmer’s house, a young village girl came over to give Qi Wen a few eggs and other miscellaneous items, making Qi Wen blush bright red, very embarrassed.

“Hahahaha… Young Taoist Qi Wen, I heard that you Taoists can get married?”

The old farmer who was helping Qi Wen slaughter a chicken laughed, and asked the little Taoist priest with a smile.

Qi Wen had grown up by now, and his face was quite handsome. While practicing his health-preserving exercises, he had also been traveling with his master all year round. He had also been beaten by unruly customers from time to time, so his body was very robust.1 He was quite popular with country ladies.

“Pro-probably, yes…”

“Hahaha, hey, little Taoist priest, if you can return to secular life and marry into another family, with your strength and looks, there are plenty of excellent girls in the village!”

There were also a few girls secretly peeking at Qi Wen and giggling from outside the fence of the old farmer’s house. Qi Wen blushed harder.

“Done!”

After thoroughly plucking the feathers and cleaning the guts, the old farmer wrapped the chicken and tossed it into the basket on the ground.

“Thank you, Uncle Shen. Here are the coins!”

Qi Wen handed over ten coins. It was much cheaper to buy chicken in the village here than in the county town. Moreover, chicken, duck, and the like was merely considered lesser meats in many places around Dazhen, while beef, mutton, and pigs were the superior meats. Naturally, unless a worker ox died of old age or some kind of accident, beef was hard to come by.

Just as the old man took the copper coins and was about to reply to Qi Wen, a voice came from outside the yard.

“Hey, hey… that’s him, that’s Taoist Qi Wen from Cloud Mountain Temple.”

“Oh, thank you so much!”

“You’re welcome, you’re welcome!”

The voice outside attracted Qi Wen’s attention. He saw two men, followed by four people who were clearly their servants, hurriedly walking into the old farmer’s yard from outside.

Qi Wen recognized one of the men: he was the one who had gotten very angry when he had asked his master to interpret his fortune the other day.

‘Oh my goodness, did he come here to fight me? ’

Qi Wen’s scalp went numb, and he glanced left and right. This was Yunkou Village. If he were beaten, the nearby villagers would probably help him out.

Fortunately, the visitor immediately stated the purpose of his visit.

“Young Taoist Priest, my name is Huang Xingye, this is my friend Li Mian. I haven’t seen your stall you set up in the county these past few days, so I came here to ask about the location of Cloud Mountain Temple. It’s great to see you! Is your master on the mountain? Thanks to your master’s reminder that day, I was able to surpass a difficult experience. I asked some vendors in front of the City God Temple, and they all said that you are Taoist priests from Yun Mountain, but they didn’t know where your temple was on that mountain. I came to this village here at the foot of the mountain to try my luck…”

The visitor said a lot in one breath. Qi Wen breathed a sigh of relief when he heard his words. He was glad the man was not here to start trouble.

“Naturally, Master is in Cloud Mountain Temple. Uh, Sir Pilgrim,2 do you need me to ask the Master to come down the mountain to see you?”

“No, no, we’ll just go to your temple to pay a visit. How can we trouble you to come down the mountain? I also brought some small gifts with me, we can take it up the mountain with us!”

It was only then that Qi Wen realized that the group of people were carrying two large loads on their shoulders. He had not noticed until just now due to his nervousness.

“Then… visiting pilgrims, please follow me up the mountain!”

Qi Wen had no choice but to bid farewell to the villagers, then guide the group of people up the mountain. It was rare for Cloud Mountain Temple to see such country gentlemen bringing things up the mountain.

On the way, the man spoke with Qi Wen about the specific reasons for his visit.

It turned out that Mr. Huang was considered the head of one of the wealthier families in Dongle County. He had recently experienced a setback and almost lost his life, but fortunately he had found Taoist Qingsong, who had interpreted his fortune in front of Dongle County’s City God Temple that day.

Although he had been very angry that time, Taoist Qingsong at the very least had succeeded in making the man more vigilant to things around him, particularly when the Taoist had mentioned “men’s hidden intentions.”3 Combined with his fortune, it at least proved the accuracy of the man’s suspicions.

In addition, to honor the seemingly perfunctory words of reminding him to do good deeds to avert disaster, the man simply did a good deed after arriving in the town. He paid the remainder of the funds missing in order to be able to renovate the Land God Temple in one go, and even personally purchased and delivered an incense burner.

To be clear, most incense burners were made of copper, and copper was what money was made out of, which made it very valuable. These two sums of money could greatly accelerate and improve the renovation of the Land God Temple.

During that period of time, the man was even more cautious in his every action than before, but even so, he almost didn’t survive when disaster struck.

I didn’t expect the other party to be so desperate that he would dare set fire to his friend’s house while he was staying there overnight.

Strangely enough, that night, when Huang Xingye and his friend who knew martial arts were sharing a room at the friend’s farm, a servant had been standing guard outside. In the middle of the night, a teapot on the table suddenly fell to the ground, waking both Huang Xingye and his friend.

At that moment, they could smell smoke coming from outside. When the two rushed out, they found that the fire had already started. One of the Huang family’s servants was unconscious outside of the house, and the other had disappeared.

The two men immediately woke their friends and family members, carried the Huang family’s servant out, and then found someone to put out the fire. In the end, although the fire had not been extinguished, at least no one had been hurt.

Afterwards, Huang Xingye and his friend reported the case to the police. Because they had a clear account, and with the help of their family wealth, the county government made great efforts to solve the case, stirring up a lot of excitement, from Dongle County’s capital all the way to Maoqian Town.

Although Huang Xingye didn’t realize it at first, after the fire, he grew more and more certain that the broken teapot had been a life-saving signal. The more he thought about it, the more mysterious it seemed. His friend ‘s family was not poor, and all the tables and chairs in the room were stable. The teapot would not have fallen for no reason, so he could only believe that it was the aid of ghosts or gods.

He also believed that it just happened to be in response to his donation to the Land God Temple.

Today, Huang Xingye came to express his gratitude, but that did not mean the problem had been solved. On the contrary, there were still some odd and difficult points. That was why he had thought of looking for the Taoist who had interpreted his fortune that day.

Before coming here, Huang Xingye had inquired about the fortune-teller in Dongle County. In the end, he found that although this fortune-teller was sometimes disliked, and even beaten due to his fortune-telling, his predictions were truly accurate. Even those who had beaten him had no choice but to admit it in the end.

Strangely enough, although this Taoist had been beaten several times, he still enjoyed setting up a stall. This seemed a bit unusual to an intelligent man like Huang Xingye. A skilled Taoist who behaved so strangely… Could he be some hidden master?

T/n: LOL

The word used to refer to the man roughly translates to “this believer”

心中 (xīnzhōng): central point/in one’s thoughts/in one’s heart, 方向 (fāngxiàng) direction/orientation. A more literal translation would be “the direction of one’s heart,” but it refers vaguely to the implicit understanding that you can never know other people’s hearts.

Chapter 162: The Transformation of a Man-Made Disaster

Almost an hour had passed, but the group still had not yet reached the foot of Yanxia Peak. They were still walking up Yun Mountain, but much slower than Qi Wen’s pace when he came down.

Qi Wen was already used to walking around Yun Mountain, but for Huang Xingye, the mountain trail was bumpy and not very accessible for his four servants, especially as they were carrying things. Li Mian was much better off.

Li Mian was a martial artist, after all. Although he was not a martial arts master, he could still be considered an important figure in Dongle County. Of the officials in the county government, only the county Captain was more skilled than him. Other constables could not match Li Mian in an individual fight, unless they fought desperately.

Looking at Qi Wen who was walking briskly in front of him, then at the baskets of items trailing behind him, in front of him, Li Mian couldn’t help but sigh.

“Does this young Taoist priest really not know martial arts? His physique certainly does not show that he is accustomed to walking on mountain trails!”

Huang Xingye looked at the loads carried by the servants. Two bundles were shared between the four of them. The baskets hung on their shoulder poles were not big, containing cloth, brocade, wine, and cakes. The weight of everything combined likely did not add up to the weight of Qi Wen’s basket. After all, Qi Wen’s basket pack was filled with bacon, eggs, pumpkins, taro, and other produce from the farmers, all of which were quite the heavy goods.

“Haah…haah… it’s not strange, it’s not too unusual. The more unusual Cloud Mountain Temple… haah… The more… The more it proves that we made the right choice… haah…”

Huang Xingye was getting older; although he hadn’t gained much weight, his physical strength was a little poor. After climbing the mountain for so long, he was out of breath.

Qi Wen still felt quite relaxed at the moment. In truth, if it had been him from the past, he would also have been very tired after carrying heavy loads for such a long time. However, he recently felt that his physical strength had greatly improved, and even going down the mountain to fetch water was accomplished at a brisk pace.

After climbing up the gentle slope, they finally reached the foot of Yanxia Peak. Qi Wen turned around excitedly and shouted at the group a couple feet behind him.

“Everyone, hurry up, we have reached Yanxia Peak, we can start hiking now!”

Hm? They could start climbing now?

So… what were they doing up until now?

Huang Xingye and others were a little stunned. They thought they were almost at Cloud Mountain Temple, but they didn’t expect him to say something like that. They gazed up at the towering Yanxia Peak and swallowed subconsciously.

Without considering any altitude calculations or their current height on the hillside, just from where Qi Wen was standing, the total height of Yanxia Peak was about 150 zhang, with Cloud Mountain Temple located about 100 zhang up.1

Although it wasn’t really all that high, the trail’s slope was much steeper. They walked and stopped for rest many times during the climb. By the time they approached Cloud Mountain Temple, Huang Xingye and his servants were already exhausted. Even Li Mian, who had been supporting Huang Xingye, was sweating profusely.

Qi Wen wiped the sweat from his forehead with his sleeve cuff, and shouted excitedly towards the Taoist temple.

“Master, Mr. Ji! We have guests!”

After a few shouts, Taoist Qingsong and Ji Yuan, who had already heard the noise and movement outside, came out of the temple together.

“Hahaha… A group of pilgrims have come from afar. I apologize for not being here to welcome you!”

“Please, please, come to Cloud Mountain Temple to rest!”

Taoist Qingsong greeted the group and walked forward with Ji Yuan. One by one, he took over the load from the servants and helped them carry it into the temple. This was not a place to refuse gifts.

Inside the temple, Ji Yuan and the two Taoists brought out several stools and chairs from the kitchen to the adjoining room, and ushered the guests over to sit and rest in the kitchen.

In addition to the tea from the Taoist Temple, they also took out some of the snacks brought by Huang Xingye and his group, and ate them together.

They had consumed a lot of physical energy, so they were too busy to stand on ceremony, even with the gifts they had brought.

After the visitors had rested for a while, particularly Huang Xingye, who had recovered his breath, Taoist Qingsong asked them about their purpose in coming here.

“I wonder, why have the pilgrims before me come to visit my Cloud Mountain Temple? I heard from from Qi Wen that you came to express your gratitude. This gift is a bit much.”

Huang Xingye sorted out his thoughts. This time, his words were quite different from what he had said to Qi Wen before. He opened his mouth and asked for help.

“Master Qingsong, you must save me! I’m in big trouble!”

Huang Xingye stood up and bowed with cupped hands repeatedly while begging.

“Mr. Huang, what happened?”

Taoist Qingsong, who hurriedly stood up to support Huang Xingye, was also surprised. Ji Yuan, who was sitting near the kitchen door, frowned.

“Taoist Master, I would first like to thank you for the previous matter. You saved my life when you helped interpret the fortune for me that day…”

Huang Xingye first reiterated what he had said to Qi Wen on the road before, but this time with much more detail. He even included the fact that he suspected the Land God had saved his life.

“Since Mr. Huang has already reported the case to the officials, and the county government is making every effort to investigate, then even if the main culprit temporarily escapes, it should not be a threat to you.”

Taoist Qingsong was a little puzzled.

“Oh, Taoist Master, it would be wonderful if that were the case! But, some time ago, I felt grateful for the Land God’s help, so I went to the Land God Temple to express my gratitude! Although the temple in our town had not yet been repaired, the statue of the Land God had been erected, so I offered his a feast of fish and red meat…”

So far it all seemed good, but the people around him who didn’t know what had happened were listening in confusion. Huang Xingye paused, then continued.

“After I gave the offerings, I used jiaobei2 to ask the Land God whether the danger had passed… However, the jiaobei fell to the ground three times without once showing a positive divine response.3 Anxious, I threw the jiaobei six more times in succession. After a total of nine attempts, there was still no affirmation. On the tenth attempt, one of the jiaobei blocks even chipped…”

“Hiss……”

At his words, Taoist Qingsong gasped. He was well versed in divination, and he knew better than Huang Xingye how ominous this result was. If Ji Yuan had not been sitting next to him, he might have lost his composure by now.

Like the fortune sticks, the jiaobei was a common divination tool found in temples. It was usually made from the roots of a golden bamboo, split in half. Sometimes, it would be replaced by two shells of a clam. On average, it was about one finger long and two fingers wide.

It was often used in conjunction with fortune sticks, or simply to divine good or bad luck. It was a simple way for pilgrims to commune with the gods. Some results would be effective while others would not. This was dependent on the gods themselves and the piety of the pilgrim.

The pilgrims would drop the jiaobei from a height of several feet. If one side faced up while the other faced down, it represented the harmony of Yin and Yang, which represented a shengyao, a “divine affirmation” or “auspicious” answer. If both sides were facing up, or both were facing down, it was an “ominous” sign.4

In Huang Xingye’s case, it was obviously impossible for the Land God to fail to respond.

“So, because the Land God responded to you with a sign of danger, Mr. Huang came to me to help you more carefully figure it out?”

Taoist Qingsong analyzed it for a while, but saw Huang Xingye shaking his head uneasily.

“More than that, more than that… The Land God…”

At this point, Huang Xingye showed some lingering fear, and spoke in a more nervous tone.

“The next day, I returned to the Land God temple to burn incense in a restless mood. I found that many people in the town were gathered in front of the temple. After inquiring, I learned that the Land God’s statue had been damaged the night before. I went into the temple and saw that the Land God’s left hand bad been torn off…”

As Huang Xingye related this, his expression was extremely ugly. At that moment, he suddenly knew that the statue of the Lord Land God was definitely not destroyed by bandits as the villagers had gossiped about, but was most likely destroyed by some terrible entity.

When everyone present heard Huang Xingye’s words, they all felt a chill rise from the soles of their feet to the top of their heads. Even people like Li Mian, who had already heard the story, felt the same way.

Taoist Qingsong’s face turned even paler. Even if he was usually rather careless with is life, he knew that this matter was definitely beyond his ability.

“Mr. Huang, this… Cloud Mountain Temple is just a small temple in the mountains, and I am just a poor Taoist who tells fortunes. How could I interfere with your affairs…”

Huang Xingye’s expression grew even more distorted, and he kept bowing and begging.

“Master Qingsong, I know that I was blind at the beginning. You actually wanted to intervene in front of the temple that day, but I didn’t listen and had a bad attitude, so I left. Please give me another chance. I, Huang, will never forget your kindness!”

Taoist Qingsong also panicked. This matter was too strange. He even forgot that the great god Ji Yuan was sitting right next to him.

“Mr. Huang, you misunderstood, I…”

However, before Qi Xuan could finish speaking, Ji Yuan, who had a serious face, spoke out loud for the first time.

“May I ask Mr. Huang if he has been to the City God Temple of Dongle County to ask the City God for aid?”

Ji Yuan’s voice was clear and strong, and it seemed to have some kind of serene power, calming the emotions of everyone present.

There was something strange about all of this. How could Huang Xingye, a mortal, still be alive and well when the opponent had the ability to attack the Land God?

Multiply by 10 to get the distance in feet. Multiply by 3.3 to get the distance in meters.

Jiaobei, also known as moon blocks are wooden divination tools thrown three times, in pairs, to divine the answer to a yes/no question.

聖筊 (shèngjiǎo or shèng yáo): divine answer “yes,” one block flat and the other upside-down.

This is actually slightly off: if both sides are facing down, that is a “laughing” response, in that the gods are “laughing” at you in the way the curved pieces rock on the floor. This can be interpreted as a “dumb question” response OR an an EMPHATIC “NO”

Chapter 163: I Will Not Sit Idly By

Ji Yuan had originally heard from Qi Wen that Huang Xingye’s matter had been resolved. This time, it originally seemed that he had come to express his gratitude, so Ji Yuan decided to listen in on a whim.

However, after hearing about what happened to the Land God, Ji Yuan knew that this matter was extraordinary, shifting from a simple man-made disaster to something more sinister.

At this moment, Ji Yuan’s widened his supernatural eyes three-quarters of the way open. He observed Huang Xingye and the people accompanying him closely. The aura and vitality of the group, which originally appeared rather vague in his eyes, suddenly became more solid, and the colors became richer.

Ji Yuan discovered that Huang Xingye himself was fine, but, although the scale of the vitality and aura of the servants had not changed, there was an aura of blood and death hidden within the rising vital Qi.

After hearing Ji Yuan’s sudden words, Huang Xingye calmed down, and turned to Taoist Qingsong to ask:

“Master Qingsong, who is this?”

“This is Mr. Ji, a great benefactor of ours…”

Qi Wen hurriedly went to answer, but Ji Yuan immediately interrupted him. There was no need laud his actions so much in front of outsiders.1

“I’m a friend of Taoist Master Qingsong. I met him a few years ago while traveling in other provinces. I am currently temporarily staying in Cloud Mountain Temple.”

“Yes, yes, Mr. Ji is an honored guest of my temple. His question just now was exactly what I wanted to ask. Has Mr. Huang gone to the City God Temple in Dongle County to pray for aid?”

In this era, even if people had never personally seen ghosts or gods, they generally believed in them, and were even quite superstitious about certain things. Huang Xingye followed this trend, so he would likely have some answer for them.

Huang Xingye’s emotions had stabilized a lot by this time, and his breathing had slowed from its previous excited state. He calmed himself further before answering.

“Of course, I went to the City God Temple in Dongle County, and it was there that I learned that you, Taoist Qingsong, were a Taoist priest from Yun Mountain. I paid for the repair of the Land God statue in my hometown, and brought sufficient offerings for the Lord City God, then I came all the way to the foot of Yun Mountain…”

Ji Yuan took another look at Huang Xingye. This person was quite smart, he was able to consider various paths of action. Then, Ji Yuan turned to Taoist Qingsong and spoke very casually.

“Taoist Qingsong, I heard you say that Lord Land God has not had a temple built for him yet, so his own spiritual levels should not be too high. At the very least, he has not received incense power or ascended to become a true god yet.”

Ji Yuan additionally thought to himself that the Land God had likely not cultivated and practiced connecting to the earth’s veins enough to “become a god through power,” otherwise he wouldn’t have responded personally so many times purely due to Huang Xingye’s significant contributions to the temple. However, the Land God obviously did not anticipate what might happen afterwards.2

Daoist Qingsong was stunned for a moment. Did he say such a thing?

However, seeing Ji Yuan asking him so seriously, Qi Xuan immediately reacted.

“Er… That’s true, what Mr. Ji said is correct.”

Ji Yuan nodded.

“It seems that I remembered correctly. From my perspective, if the Land God’s statue was destroyed by humans, then we would naturally increase the investigation efforts. If there truly were an evil being nearby, Maoqian Town is also under the jurisdiction of Dongle County. Even if Mr. Huang did not offer tribute, as long as he knew about this matter, the City God would not sit idly by. Am I right, Taoist priest?”

Taoist Qingsong nodded repeatedly.

“Yes, yes, Mr. Ji is right!”

“How wonderful, truly good to hear.”

Huang Xingye felt relieved at his words. Although ordinary people believed in entities like the City God and Land God, almost no one had ever seen them. Some so-called miraculous rumors were just word-of-mouth. It was impossible to be sure whether something had really happened. It was more reassuring to have a master like Taoist Qingsong, who could be physically sensed.

“By the way, Mr. Huang, about what you just said… The dangers in the first half of your story are almost all man-made. After you discovered the truth and reported the evidence to the police, nothing unusual happened. Was it only after you received the divination response from the Land God that you realized something was wrong?”

Huang Xingye bowed to Ji Yuan before answering.

“As Mr. Ji said, I thought that the disaster was over, and sent offerings to thank the Lord Land God.”

To be able to be friends with a master like Taoist Qingsong, this Mr. Ji must have some special qualities. At the very least, his calm and composure were already extraordinary.

Ji Yuan looked at Taoist Qingsong, who subconsciously looked back at him.

“Taoist master, don’t you also think this is quite strange?”

“Yes, as Mr. Ji said, I also think so. It’s quite fortunate that Mr. Huang was able to arrive here unharmed!”

Taoist Qingsong not only echoed Ji Yuan, but also began to take a closer look at Huang Xingye’s current face and expression. Based on some of the things he had heard described before, the Taoist noticed some odd points in his divination.

“Mr. Huang, could you please tell me your birth date?”

With Ji Yuan by his side, Taoist Qingsong became more courageous.

When Huang Xingye heard that Taoist Qingsong was going to tell his fortune, he naturally did not refuse further, and hurriedly whispered his birth date and birth hour to the master.

After hearing the man’s eight characters, the latter pondered over it carefully in his mind, pinching his fingers together from time to time to assist in calculating based on the Heavenly Stems,3 Earthly Branches,4 and the movement of the stars.5

Ji Yuan watched curiously, while Huang Xingye and others waited nervously.

In fact, Huang Xingye came from a wealthy family, and thus had experienced his fortune being read multiple times since he was a child. The results were basically the same each time: nothing more than a good life and good fortune.

After a long while, Taoist Qingsong finally examined Huang Xingye’s current expression and spoke.

“Boss Huang was blessed with profound fortune in his destiny, along with the blessings of his ancestors. As long as you dared to forge ahead and were not lazy, the first half of your life would be smooth sailing, and the second half of your life should be free of worry… However…”

“The Star of Ketu now shines upon Mr. Huang.6 Originally, with such profound blessings, nothing major would have happened. But now that you have encountered this matter, the man-made disaster has turned into a demonic one, and it is a fatal calamity!”

Huang Xingye’s expression darkened as he waited for Taoist Qingsong to continue.

“Although you have a good destiny, people’s fate is not fixed. Your knowledge, experience, good deeds and evil deeds, and all other kinds of external factors can impact it. Now, it seems that there is a strange energy hidden in your fate, almost like a living being. The last time I observed your face, it was different. Only now, when I could combine your eight characters to calculate your fortune…”

The others in the group were anxious, and could not understand Taoist Qingsong’s words, but Ji Yuan listened carefully, all the while observing Huang Xingye carefully with his supernatural eyes.

Combined with the emotions Huang Xingye expressed upon hearing Taoist Qingsong’s words, his aura had begun to change. There was actually a strange feeling, like a loach swimming in the air in his aura, very easy to overlook if one didn’t observe carefully.

Taoist Qingsong was just a fortune teller, so naturally he finished what he had to say.

“As long as Mr. Huang survives this year’s calamity, you will be blessed by the Moon next year. This year’s crisis will create a recoil. As long as your enterprising spirit remains unchanged, you will have good luck, and be able to accomplish anything.”

A smile appeared on Huang Xingye’s face.

“But you must first surpass this obstacle!”

Ji Yuan heard his words and once more pretended to ask.

“Taoist Qingsong, you once told me about strange things like demons and evil spirits. You said that some demons and evil spirits prey on the special fated Qi of people with good fortune. They are afraid that the Qi of destiny will dissipate, so they guide these people into traps. Doesn’t it seem a bit like that situation now?”

Hearing Ji Yuan’s words, Taoist Qingsong just nodded.

“Mr. Ji is right. It definitely resembles that, it looks quite similar!”

Ji Yuan nodded and continued.

“I estimate that the evil being probably didn’t really want to burn Mr. Huang to death. It just wished to severely harm you to a state of near death, then pursue some other avenue. Otherwise, its goals could not be achieved if you died and it lost all of that luck, right, Taoist priest?”

“Yes, as Mr. Ji said. But being in a state of near-death is even worse…”

Taoist Qingsong echoed, and did not forget to express his own ideas, while Ji Yuan continued to add on more sentences.

“There are some evil demons and lurking in this world. They are indeed quite strange. Those who are more cunning and powerful can take advantage of loopholes in the gods’ domains. It seems that they will not let Mr. Huang go.”

Seeing Huang Xingye shrink his neck and look even worse, Ji Yuan also comforted him.

“But, Mr. Huang, please rest assured. Even if the Lord City God cannot do anything to control the outcome. Taoist Qingsong will definitely not sit idly by. Right, Daoist?”

“That’s right, let’s first… uh…”

Qi Xuan suddenly reacted halfway through nodding his head. He stared blankly at Ji Yuan. Seeing the latter nod to reassure him, he grit his teeth and continued to speak.

“Yes, although I am not very experienced, since Mr. Huang has come all this way to ask for my aid, I cannot ignore it!”

Huang Xingye was pleasantly surprised. He had come up the mountain to beg for his outcome, so he quickly stood up and bowed to express his gratitude. His servants and his friend Li Mian bowed together with him.

“Thank you, Taoist Qingsong, for your help! Thank you, Taoist Qingsong, for your help! If I can survive this calamity, I will definitely reward you handsomely!”

T/n: Introducing Ji Yuan, gaslighter extraordinaire, and Taoist Qingsong, BS-er extraordinaire. Truly a top 10 anime team-up

Lit: “add too much color to his own affairs”

Several vague statements here. 1) The Land God here hasn’t yet achieved a high enough cultivation level to become a “True Deity.” 2) JY knows this because, if he were a True Deity, he wouldn’t bother with acknowledging HXY’s contributions. 3) JY also knows because (as stated in the prev. paragraph) the Land God didn’t have a temple until HXY built one for him. 4) Finally, something happened that even the Land God did not expect as a result of him responding to HXY.

We have encountered Heavenly Stems before, but it’s essentially a system of ordered symbols with a variety of uses. Used in conjunction with the Earthly Branches, it forms a sexagenary cycle, a cycle of 60 years used for recording time in Ancient China.

The Earthly Branches are a set of 12 ordered symbols used in cosmology. You can think of it in its most basic form kind of like the 4 cardinal directions, except there are 12 of them. Used in conjunction with the Heavenly Stems, it forms a sexagenary cycle, a cycle of 60 years used for recording time in Ancient China.

Here, Taoist Qingsong is quite literally calculating the guy’s future based on the exact position of the stars at his birth, which he can tell based on the date and time of the man’s birth.

计都 (jìdū): Ketu in Sanskrit, refers to the farthest point on the moon, or the last point in the moon’s orbit, later changed to the intersection of the moon and the ecliptic. Has spiritual implications, including enlightenment and liberation, but more often associated with presaging disaster/loss on the material plane

Chapter 164: The Land God In Hiding

The sky was gradually darkening, and it was unrealistic to ask Huang Xingye and his group to head down the mountain at this time.

Taoist Qingsong went into the kitchen to cook again, using the items Qi Wen had carried over, along with some of the things Huang Xingye had brought, as ingredients to make enough dinner for everyone to eat.

Perhaps because he had found Taoist Qingsong, Huang Xingye felt an unprecedented sense of security in Cloud Mountain Temple. He felt more relaxed, particularly after having spoken to Taoist Master Qingsong that afternoon.

Huang Xingye, who had not had much of an appetite in recent days, ate a lot that night, and even had a few drinks.

As for the problem of accommodation, there was an easy solution. Ji Yuan simply gave up his own room. There was enough straw bedding inside for six people to sleep. Huang Xingye was not the kind of person to be concerned about a master and his servants sharing the same space.

Ji Yuan naturally went to the two Taoists’ room and shared their bedding.

When everyone including Qi Wen in their room had fallen asleep, Taoist Qingsong, who was feeling the most uneasy out of all of the residents of the temple, finally couldn’t hold back any longer, and began to ask Ji Yuan in a low voice.

“Mr. Ji, I know you are still awake. You must have thought of a way to help Huang Xingye, right?”

Of course, Ji Yuan indeed had not gone to sleep, and he knew that Taoist Qingsong would not be able to sleep either. Upon hearing Qi Xuan’s nervous question, he joked.

“Of course, I have come up with a countermeasure. The solution is naturally for Taoist Qingsong to go down the mountain with Huang Xingye. If there are any variables, he can help him slay the demons and eliminate the monsters.”

Taoist Qingsong’s heart trembled when he heard these words.

“Oh… Mr. Ji, you know how strong I am. I can only tell people’s fortunes. I don’t know how to slay demons or eliminate monsters… I’ve never even seen a ghost before, isn’t this a bit too difficult? I have only just recuperated my lifespan, what if I die directly after all this…”

Ji Yuan didn’t try scare him anymore. He had previously asked Taoist Qingsong to agree with him, so he naturally had his own plans.

“Hahaha… Taoist Qingsong will definitely go down the mountain with Huang Xingye, but it may not be the real Taoist Qingsong.”

After saying these words in a profound and inscrutable manner, Ji Yuan’s spiritual energy flowed slightly, and some changes began to occur in his body. His nose became a little wider and a little flatter, the corners of his eyebrows elongated and angled a little. He grew crow’s feet wrinkles at the corners of his eyes. Even the skin tone, musculature, and mole placement on his face all changed.

In the span of two breaths, two Taoist Qingsongs faced each other.

The stars were bright tonight, with a shining moon hanging high in the sky. With starlight shining through the windows, a certain degree of visibility could be maintained after the human eye had adapted to the darkness.

At this moment, Taoist Qingsong widened his eyes and breathed in sharply, speechless.

Ji Yuan had not yet learned a true transformation technique. In truth, the transformation technique in the Old Dragon’s jade slip was not very suitable for humans. Ji Yuan still needed to comprehend it carefully and find a way to modify it before he could learn it.

Essentially, this kind of transformation was incomparable to Sun Wukong’s seventy-two transformations.1 It contained the influence of the concealment technique. At present, it was only possible for him to draw a bit of information from it.

In addition, Ji Yuan and Taoist Qingsong were similar in size, so he used his excellent concealment skill to achieve a kind of “face changing” technique.

The characteristics of his own spiritual power were quite mysterious. There was almost no fluctuation of power when it was not actively being used. By referencing some of the “transformation” techniques, it made it extremely difficult to see through this kind of “face changing” technique. He then put on Taoist Qingsong’s Taoist robe, and changed his hairstyle.

By now, he estimated that only Qi Wen, who lived with Taoist Qingsong day and night, could point out the differences. No one else would be able to see it, not even Huang Xingye, any of the people outside, or even ordinary ghosts and gods.

Strictly speaking, his current transformation was similar to a combination of spiritual energy techniques and martial arts disguise and makeup, but the traces of each were extremely faint.

Early the next morning, ignoring Qi Wen’s terrified expression as if he had seen a ghost, the two Taoist priests surnamed Qi from the temple escorted the guests back down the mountain. Finally, Taoist priest Qingsong alone continued with Huang Xingye and his group and they left the mountain.

As for the Mr. Ji from yesterday, it seemed as though he had caught a cold, and did not get up to send them off.

In Cloud Mountain Temple, Taoist Qingsong, who had been hiding in the house, waited for everyone to go down the mountain. It was a long time before Qi Wen returned.

“Master, I’m back!”

Qi Wen hurriedly ran back. When he entered the hall, he saw Qi Xuan, who was drinking tea, and let out a sigh of relief.

“Master, why does Mr. Ji’s face look the same as yours? Is this the disguise technique of a martial arts master? Can you teach me too?”

Taoist Qingsong slapped Qi Wen lightly on the head.

“What kind of disguise are you talking about? It’s an immortal technique. How many times have I told you, Mr. Ji is an immortal.”

Qi Wen rubbed his forehead subconsciously and muttered.

“Mr. Ji himself said that he is not…”

. . .

After leaving the Yun Mountain range, the trail became easier to travel on. In other words, either there was a clear road under their feet, or the path was relatively flat.

Huang Xingye and others had left a horse-drawn carriage in Yunkou Village. They couldn’t use it when they went up the mountain the day before, but now they naturally didn’t have to walk all the way back on their feet when they returned to their hometown today.

There were two carriages in total, one with a canopy and the other with a flatbed cart. They did not return to Dongle County’s capital, but instead went directly to Maoqian Town.

On the carriage, Huang Xingye began to feel uneasy again.

“Master Qingsong, would it be better for us to go to the City God Temple first?”

“No need. Since you have already given offerings to the City God, the City God of Dongle County must already know about your situation by now. He may have already sent ghosts and spirits to inspect the Land God Temple in Maoqian Town.”

While Ji Yuan spoke, his eyes kept gazing outside through the curtain of the carriage, but he didn’t see anything unusual.

“But Taoist Qingsong, wouldn’t it be safer if we hid in the county capital? My wife’s family is in the city, and several children are staying with her temporarily…”

“Mr. Huang did the right thing, but you cannot hide away and avoid it. We still need to face this matter. Even ghosts and gods make mistakes sometimes, and ordinary people even more so. Even if we stay in Dongle County’s capital forever, we cannot guarantee our safety. It would be better to solve this matter thoroughly.”

“Ah, the Taoist master is right!”

After saying these few words, Ji Yuan just closed his eyes and rested his mind, pretending not to want to say anything more. Even though Huang Xingye was nervous, he had no choice but to speak softly to Li Mian beside him.

Around noon, the carriage and horses arrived outside Maoqian Town. The servant driving the carriage informed his passengers.

“Master, we have almost arrived home.”

Before Huang Xingye could speak, he saw Taoist Qingsong, who was sitting across from him, open his eyes.

“Let’s go to see the Land God Temple first.”

“Ah! Ah Fu, head to the Land God Temple!”

“Yes, sir!”

The servant replied and adjusted the direction of the carriage. After a while, the carriage stopped outside the Land God Temple. Ji Yuan, Huang Xingye, and others got off the carriage one after another. Before the servant could come forward, Li Mian, Huang Xingye, and “Master Qingsong” walked over together.

The Land God Temple was located on the south side of Maoqian Town. Apart from the craftsman hired by the Huang family and the old men in the town who were preparing for the construction of the temple, there were no other villagers around.

Ji Yuan stopped a dozen steps outside the temple, and whispered to Huang Xingye and Li Mian.

“There is a daytime patrol in front of the temple, don’t say any unnecessary words.”

“Daytime patrol?”

Huang Xingye asked cautiously.

“It’s the so-called Sun-wandering Spirits. They should have been sent by the Lord City God of Dongle County.”

“Oh, oh! I understand, I understand!”

Huang Xingye felt relieved, while Li Mian beside him was full of doubts. He looked towards the other end of the temple, but couldn’t see anything out of the ordinary.

The three of them arrived at the Land God Temple together. The craftsmen and the old man in charge of the temple hurriedly greeted Huang Xingye, which naturally attracted the attention of the two daytime patrol officers. They all looked at Huang Xingye together.

“This person is Huang Xingye.”

“That’s right. He went to the City God Temple to offer sacrifices and report the situation here.”

“Then who is this Taoist?”

“It seems to be Taoist Qingsong from Cloud Mountain Temple, the one who often sets up a stall in front of the temple.”

While the two daytime patrol officers were talking, they scrutinized Huang Xingye carefully. Even though Ji Yuan noticed them doing so, he didn’t inform Huang Xingye, to avoid him making a fuss.

After carefully observing the statue in the temple, Ji Yuan discovered that the statue had many non-human characteristics. At present, the incense power was quite weak, and the residual divine aura was not strong. It was obvious that the Land God had not been to the Land God Temple in the past two days.

He glanced at the arm of the statue in the corner. The master craftsman was filling up the soil to support it, but he hadn’t yet affixed it back to the statue. There was no lingering evil aura, and now it looked as though it had merely been knocked off by some human.

‘Was it just a man-made disaster? ’

After watching for a while, Ji Yuan, Huang Xingye, and the others went back to the Huang Family Residence in the town, and the two daytime patrol officers followed.

Naturally, the Huang Residence was not as expansive as the Wang Mansion or Chu Residence in the Capital Prefecture. However, it was definitely a wealthy family’s mansion for Dongle County.

Due to the arrival of Taoist Qingsong, Huang Xingye was prepared to entertain him with fine wine and good food.

However, before the lunch, Ji Yuan asked the Huang family’s servants to prepare two small wooden plaques, then had some food and wine delivered to the guest house prepared by the Huang family. Then, he entered the guest house alone, and specifically told the Huang family not to disturb him for the time being.

Huang Xingye had no choice but to go out to dinner with Li Mian and the rest of his family.

In the guest house, Ji Yuan picked up a brush, and wrote “The Right Messenger of the Daytime Patrol of Dongle County” and “The Left Messenger of the Daytime Patrol of Dongle County” on each wooden plaque, respectively.

Then, he placed three sticks of sandalwood in a large bowl filled with raw rice grains and lit them.

After a while, the two daytime patrol officers were drawn over. They understood what was happening as soon as they saw the scene.

“Taoist Qingsong, can you actually see us?”

Seeing that one of the daytime patrol officers had asked him a question, Ji Yuan bowed to them.

“There were too many mortals around before. I know the rules of the underworld, so I did not contact the two envoys. I hope you can forgive me.”

The two daytime patrol officers looked the man up and down before speaking.

“Well, I can’t blame you for waiting. This table is quite rich in offerings.”

“I was just about to invite the two of you to enjoy it together!”

“Haha, wonderful!”

The two daytime patrol officers smiled. In the county’s small City God Temple, the daytime and nighttime patrols actually did not have a solid manifestation. They seldom had time to accumulate offerings, so they rarely were able to enjoy rich wines and sumptuous food.

Ji Yuan and the two patrol officers ate as they chatted. Ghosts’ and gods’ act of eating energy did not conflict with his manner of eating vegetables. It was simply that some of the dishes would appear to be untouched after the meal.

During the conversation, he learned that there had been patrols in Maoqian Town day and night for the past two days, but they had mainly been searching for the Land God. It seemed that the Land God had hidden himself so thoroughly that even the messengers from the underworld couldn’t find him.

“Is this true?”

Ji Yuan was surprised to hear this. Did the Land God hide himself away?

“It’s absolutely true. It’s precisely because of this strange matter that despite the underworld not finding any traces of evil spirits, they still have continued to send people here.”

The food and wine had been finished by now. Ji Yuan frowned and thought for a while before speaking to the two ghost messengers.

“Daytime patrol officers, I believe this matter is not so simple. I hope you can go back and report to the Lord City God right now. Tell him that Huang Xingye has a special fate, and it seems that some evil being is plotting something. I hope the underworld will send additional personnel to investigate.”

The two daytime patrol offices looked at each other.

“We will go back and report to the City God later, and wait for the Lord City God to make a decision.”

“Thank you for taking the trouble to do so!”

Ji Yuan bowed and saw the two daytime patrol officers off. After waiting for a while, his eyes flashed, and he lifted his foot and stomped lightly on the floor of the guest house.

The ground rippled in arcs like waves of water.

“I ask for an audience with the Land God of Maoqian Town!”

As long as he was connected to this land vein and wished to become the Land God, he could not escape this “Spirit Binding” no matter how hard he tried.

A puff of blue smoke swirled upwards from the ground, and a hunchbacked “person” in a yellow earth-toned robe appeared in front of Ji Yuan. His faced was covered in yellow hair, but he had human facial features.

As soon as he saw Ji Yuan, the Land God hurriedly bowed and greeted him.

“The Land God of Maoqian Town pays his respects to the Immortal. I greet the Immortals! The Immortal must be a great Taoist master. It is good that you have come, it’s so good that you have come! Please help me!”

The appearance of this Land God was quite similar to Huang Xingye, the man who had gone up the mountain to seek aid yesterday, which made Ji Yuan frown again.

“Lord Land God, please sit down and talk. Why didn’t you show up when the underworld came to look for you?”

This Land God seemed to recognize the strange “Spirit Binding” technique. He seemed very confident at this moment, but insisted on standing.

“To reply to the Immortal, this Huang Xingye has provoked a powerful creature, not even the City God of Dongle County may be able to suppress it. I helped the Huang family unknowingly before. If you hadn’t come here now, I would never have dared to appear!”

In Journey to the West, the Monkey King Sun Wukong has the ability to fully transform himself into 72 different kinds of creatures. Consider the difference between an illusion and a full transformation – JY’s technique is more or less halfway between the two.

Chapter 165: Mutual Wariness

Seeing the Land God’s determined expression, which seemed to show some knowledge of the current circumstances, Ji Yuan’s suspicions grew heavier.

“What could be so extraordinary?”

The Land God lowered his voice.

“It possesses even those with no traces of wickedness within them. It looks like a human, but is actually an evil being. Its eyes are dark, and it has a long tongue that grows from its belly. It is neither ghost, nor human, nor god. I am not sure what it is, but it is really terrifying…”

As he spoke, the Land God subconsciously touched his left arm, as if he could still feel the pain of his statue being torn apart.

“When Huang Xingye came to my temple to ask for a divination that day, I actually didn’t see anything. But because the Huang family was kind to me in building the temple, I passed on spiritual energy to Huang Xingye to make his divination more accurate…”

There were two instances in which the gods would interfere with the jiaobei. The first was when the deity, such as the City God, was closely related to the person asking the question and knew a little bit about the situation. In this manner, some information could be conveyed directly.

Another instance was in the case of a “personal spirit in the human body.”1 This method would also be used when foreigners came to the City God Temple, or when it related to other gods who did not care about the trivial matters of mortals.

The human body had its own spiritual attraction, even for ordinary people. It was recorded in both folk rumors and various immortal scriptures that the human body nurtured various spiritual consciousnesses, which were responsible for things like the functioning of the internal organs of the body. However, they were usually suppressed by the individual’s main consciousness and thus were not visible.

Helping Huang Xingye toss the jiaobei was essentially helping him test his own spiritual consciousness. The spirits in the body had their own responses, which were particularly clear when there were large changes in his life. The reading would be even more accurate for people with special destinies like Huang Xingye.

“In other words, the resulting divination wasn’t actually calculated by Huang Xingye, but rather arose from his own spiritual energy?”

“Just as the Immortal said, I don’t have the spiritual energy to see the future of that strange evil, but he threw the jiaobei nine times in a row, and it was powerful. I could see that something was wrong. I even noticed that there was some special aura around Huang Xingye that was already unstable. I saw that the situation was not good, so I broke the one of the jiaobei when he tossed them for the tenth time.”

The Land God sighed and continued.

“Then, after Huang Xingye left, I was uneasy. I didn’t see anything, but the divination and Huang Xingye’s own aura were so strange, I vaguely sensed that I might be in big trouble. Sure enough, that trouble came at midnight that very night…”

Ji Yuan narrowed his eyes.

“When the statue lost its arm?”

The Land God nodded.

“My temple had not yet been built, so the door was not closed at night. That night, a strange man wearing a bamboo hat came into the temple. I thought he was just a mortal who wanted to stay in the temple overnight. As soon as he entered the temple, he stared at my statue, and in his eyes, that originally looked normal, a dark color appeared….”

“There were also strange sounds coming from his stomach. Just hearing that sound made me feel like I was trapped in miasma. He then cursed me for being nosy and rushed towards my statue. I realized something was wrong and immediately fled. If I had been a step slower, it wouldn’t have just been the statue that would have been torn apart.”

“By the way, its voice was strange. Its tongue sounded heavy, in addition to the sounds from its belly. I once encountered a long-tongued ghost in the past, so I know that this was the sound of a being hiding its long tongue in its abdomen, but this individual was definitely not a ghost! “

The Land God told Ji Yuan everything he knew and experienced, frankly stating that this thing gave him an extremely dangerous and strange feeling. He instinctively felt that the City god of Dongle County would be powerless against it as well. Since he had been directly targeted, he simply hid himself. After all, it was difficult to find the Land God once he had gone into hiding.

A long tongue hidden in its stomach, and dark pupils… The Land God was present in the statue, but the individual was still completely invisible to his senses…

Judging from the Land God’s description, things were more tricky than he expected. This kind of thing that left almost no trace but did not seem to be afraid of ghosts and gods was quite unusual, and very dangerous.

Ji Yuan could now understand to some extent the feeling many gods and monsters had when looking at him. 2

“Lord Land God, is the being you mentioned still in Maoqian Town? Can you find him?”

At the immortal’s words, the Land God shook his head.

“It shouldn’t be in Maoqian Town now. While I was in hiding, I carefully thought about the previous events. What puzzled me was that this thing seemed to be trying every means to make Huang Xingye collapse, rather than outright killing or devouring him…”

Ji Yuan sneered.

“Hah, or maybe it wants to wait for Huang Xingye to collapse before devouring him. It’s not that it doesn’t want to cause trouble, but rather that it doesn’t want to let the Qi from Huang Xingye’s special destiny escape.”

The Land God’s words reminded Ji Yuan of Huang Xingye’s obscure and unusual Qi. He had never considered this aspect when reading auras before, but it now seemed that Huang Xingye’s “bodily spirit” was the “personal god” that was only ever mentioned in rumors in “A Historical Account from Outside the Tao.”

This kind of personal spirit was considered one of the wonders of heaven and earth. It was rumored to only appear in humans, the most intelligent creatures of the world, but there were few concrete examples. It was closely related to the human spirit, but differed from it; it was the essence of the human spirit, not the whole.

To use a crude analogy, it was similar to the difference between ginseng and ginseng essence.3

This kind of inner deity was naturally different from the divinity of ordinary gods, who used the power of incense and faith. To some extent, it was more like an extremely rare deity that had been independently conceived in heaven and earth. As the saying went, there was a vast world outside the body, and a smaller but no less vast world inside the body as well; the individual’s god was a god conceived by this small world world within the body.

Within this world, due to the influence of the five essential internal organs, the five elements of Yin and Yang could be related to the mountains, rivers, and streams.

Gods in human bodies were extremely special. Although not many had been sighted, it was mentioned “A Historical Account from Outside the Tao” that personal gods were very alert. Once they sensed that something was wrong, they would either dissipate and vanish, or they would escape the body along with its soul and disappear into the universe.

At the same time, this divine spirit was closely related to the individual’s spiritual consciousness. Once a person’s spiritual consciousness was greatly affected and disoriented, this divine spirit would also be harmed.

“Huang Xingye appears to be simply a landlord and businessman, but he could actually nurture such a miraculous thing…”

Ji Yuan muttered to himself. Although there was no evidence that a “personal god” had been formed, it was still difficult to connect the idea with the Huang Xingye who had gone up the mountain to seek aid and had been scared half to death.

Although he had caught a glimpse of it on the mountain before, Ji Yuan couldn’t recognize it at the time. After all, he had never seen anything like it before, and the description in the book was vague. Only now did he realize what it was after pondering it.

“Did the Immortal notice anything?”

Hearing Ji Yuan’s mumbles, the Land God asked curiously and carefully.

“Well, I have some guesses. Lord Land God, I must ask you to please look after the area around Maoqian Town. If I find that the individual has appeared again, please notify me immediately.”

“I will do as the Immortal says!”

The Land God, which was still far from cultivating a fully human form, cupped his hands and bowed respectfully to Ji Yuan. After Ji Yuan returned the bow, he turned into green smoke and plunged into the ground. Compared to Ji Yuan, who still felt a little uneasy, the Land God was quite confident at the moment.

Ji Yuan was a little troubled. This was no longer some ordinary monster playing a trick. The other party wanted to cause Huang Xingye to mentally collapse, then wait for an opportunity to seize the “personal spirit.” Regardless of whether it wanted to devour Huang Xingye or had some other uses for him, discovering and recognize Huang Xingye’s “personal god” was already quite a difficult task. The fact that it could do so was daunting.

It was possible that the other party was already aware of anything that could pose a threat to it in Maoqian Town and even Dongle County, except for Ji Yuan.

. . .

As they were affected by Huang Xingye, the Huang family had been relatively nervous recently.

However, after the arrival of Taoist Qingsong, Huang Xingye calmed down a lot. The master’s disposition greatly influenced his servants, and the Huang family gradually settled down. Those who went out to inspect the fields, collect rent, and transport goods all performed their tasks well.

Ji Yuan stayed with the Huang family for half a month, but nothing happened. The Huang family gradually stabilized.

During this period, the underworld of Dongle County sent several judges and gods of the Yin and Yang Division to inspect Maoqian Town. Even the Land God took the initiative to appear and describe his experiences to the messengers of the underworld.

But since then, the entirety of Dongle County, including Maoqian Town, had been peaceful, as if everything had passed.

However, Huang Xingye still abided by Ji Yuan’s instructions, leaving everything to his servants and not leaving the Huang residence.

On this day, a distinguished guest came to the Huang Mansion. He was the eldest son of a wealthy family from Changchuan Prefecture who had business dealings with Huang Xingye.

Everyone in the Huang residence was busy. Huang Xingye was drinking tea and chatting in the living room with a man named Chu Mingcai.

The warm atmosphere immediately froze when Ji Yuan walked into the living room.

The very moment Ji Yuan entered the room, the originally chatty Chu Mingcai abruptly stopped talking and turned his head to stare straight at Ji Yuan, who had just come in.

“Oh, ha, let me introduce you, nephew Chu, this is Taoist Qingsong. He, too, is a distinguished guest of our residence. A while ago, I provoked some evil spirits, so we asked the Taoist to come exorcise them.

“Master Qingsong, this is…”

Ji Yuan raised his hand to stop Huang Xingye from speaking. His eyes were opened to the widest they could be. Chu Mingcai abruptly stood up from his seat, still staring at Ji Yuan.

“Sir, are you using a human body as a flesh shell?”

Ji Yuan’s eyes had already pierced the being’s concealing veil of spiritual power. They were pale and still like a deep pool. With his supernatural eyes fully opened, the hidden demonic energy within Chu Mingcai’s shell combined with his body and soul to surge visibly, but it did not lead out of his body. Only his greedy desires were evident. This was definitely not just a wisp of demonic energy possessing him, but a true demon in human form.

In the world of cultivation, the word “true” was used with extreme caution; the same was true for True Demons.

Although the demon in human form was not obvious to spot, its desires were magnified. If Ji Yuan had not felt his waves of greed and killing intent, he would not even had noticed that the “real final boss” had arrived.

Chu Mingcai also looked at Ji Yuan with great fear. He had heard that the Huang Mansion had hired a lousy Taoist priest, and he didn’t feel anything special when the man arrived. If Ji Yuan hadn’t entered the room, he would not even have sensed any spiritual traces.

But the immortal sword hanging in the air behind him and that pair of eyes that seemed to nearly absorb his soul were nothing to scoff at.

Mutually wary of each other, Ji Yuan and Chu Mingcai had one though simultaneously flash through their minds: ‘Who is this person!?’

人身 (rénshēn): person/human body, 自 (zì): oneself, 灵 (líng): spirit/alert. This could refer to people with special destinies or particular talent in cultivation, but I believe it refers to the god possessing the individual to give insight. The wording is a bit unclear.
